## Page 32

**Arabic (original):**
— ٢٦ —

لأربع خلون من تموز وطلوع النثرة لسبع عشرة ليلة خلت منه، طلوع نجم الشعرى العبور
وطلوع الطرفة لأول ليلة من آب، وطلوع الجبهة لأربع عشرة ليلة خلت منه، وطلوع الزبرة لسبع
وعشرين منه، وطلوع الصرفة لثمان خلون من أيلول، وطلوع العواء لتسع عشرة ليلة خلت منه،
وطلوع السماك لليلتين بقيتا منه، وطلوع الإكليل لاثنتي عشرة ليلة خلت من تشرين الثاني، وطلوع
القلب لخمس وعشرين منه، وطلوع الشولة لثمان عشرة من كانون أول، وطلوع النعائم لإحدى
وعشرين منه، وطلوع البلدة لثلاث خلون من كانون الثاني، وطلوع الذابح لإحدى عشرة ليلة منه،
وطلوع سعد السعود لسبع وعشرين ليلة خلت منه، وطلوع سعد الأخبية لإحدى عشرة ليلة
خلت من شباط، وطلوع سعد بلع لست وعشرين منه، وطلوع الفرغ لليلتين خلتا من
آذار، وطلوع الفرغ المؤخر لأربع وعشرين خلت منه، وطلوع الرشا لأربع من نيسان، والله أعلم.
( فصل في أقسام هذه المنازل على الفصول الأربعة )
اعلم أن لفصل الربيع الشرطين والبطين والثريا والدبران والهقعة والهنعة والذراع، ولفصل
الصيف النثرة والطرفة والجبهة والزبرة والصرفة والعواء، ولفصل الخريف الغفر والزبانان
والإكليل والقلب والشولة والنعائم والبلدة، ولفصل الشتاء سعد السعود وسعد الذابح وسعد
الأخبية وبلع والفرغان والمؤخر والرشا، ولكل فصل سبع منازل.
( فصل في أسجاع العرب المتعلقة بالمنازل وما نقل عن الأوائل )
قال بعضهم: قرأت على شيخنا الكندي رحمه الله تعالى، قال: قرأت على أبي منصور
الخولاني، قال: بلغني عن أبي محمد المناوي أنه قال: تقول العرب: إذا طلع الشرطين استوى الرمان،
واخضرت الأغصان، وعمرت الأوطان، وتهادى الجيران، وبات الفقير بكل مكان. وإذا طلع البطين
انقضى الدين. وإذا طلعت الثريا ابغ لراعيك كساءً، وإذا طلعت له غداً ابغ له سقاءً. وإذا طلع
الدبران توقدت النيران ويبست الغدران. وإذا طلعت الهقعة رجعت الناس عن الفحمة. وإذا
طلعت الهنعة انقطعوا إلى المنعة. وإذا طلع الذراع حسرت الشمس الشعاع وتوقد الشراب بكل قاع.
وإذا طلعت النثرة ضعف العجل بكثرة ولم ينل في دره قطرة. وإذا طلعت الطرفة سهل أبو الضيف بخفة.
وإذا طلعت العواء فرت الحيات وطاب الهواء. وإذا طلع السماك كثر على المال الملاك. وإذا طلع الفرغان
والنسر فاضت الغدران وسال جسر. وإذا طلع الزبانان أخذ كل معيان. وإذا طلع الإكليل بطل المقاليل.
وإذا طلع القلب هان كل صعب. وإذا طلعت الشولة أجلت الشيخ البولة. وإذا طلعت النعائم حصل النعم
إلى كل قائم. وإذا طلعت البلدة فأكلك العصيدة وهو ما تخرج من الزبد والسمن من أسفل القدر.
وإذا طلع الذابح حمى عديل الناح. وإذا طلع الأخبية حب الناس لبس الأقبية. وإذا طلع بلع صارت
الأرض لمعاً. وإذا طلع الشولا [~] رر الكلا. وإذا طلع الفرغ المقدم فاخدم ولا تندم. وإذا طلع الفرغ
المؤخر فأسرع ولا تؤخر. وإذا طلع السمكة أمكنت الحركة وتعلقت الحسكة. وهذه أسجاع العرب
ذكرنا طرفاً منها على تمام الكلام على المنازل، والله أعلم.
( الفصل الرابع من البروج الاثني عشر وما فيها من الارتباطات والإشارات )
اعلم

**English:**
— 26 —

for four nights having passed of July, and the rising of al-Nathrah for seventeen nights having passed of it, the rising of the star of al-Shi‘rā al-‘Abūr,
and the rising of al-Ṭarfah for the first night of August, and the rising of al-Jabhah for fourteen nights having passed of it, and the rising of al-Zubrah for seven
and twenty of it, and the rising of al-Ṣarfah for eight nights having passed of September, and the rising of al-‘Awā’ for nineteen nights having passed of it,
and the rising of al-Simāk for two nights remaining of it, and the rising of al-Iklīl for twelve nights having passed of November, and the rising of
al-Qalb for twenty-five of it, and the rising of al-Shawlah for eighteen of December, and the rising of al-Na‘ā’im for twenty-one
of it, and the rising of al-Baldah for three nights having passed of January, and the rising of al-Dhābiḥ for eleven nights of it,
and the rising of Sa‘d al-Su‘ūd for twenty-seven nights having passed of it, and the rising of Sa‘d al-Akhbiyah for eleven nights
having passed of February, and the rising of Sa‘d Bulā‘ for twenty-six of it, and the rising of al-Fargh for two nights having passed of
March, and the rising of al-Fargh al-Mu’akhkhar for twenty-four having passed of it, and the rising of al-Rashā for four of April, and Allah knows best.
(Section on the divisions of these mansions according to the four seasons)
Know that for the season of spring are al-Sharṭayn, al-Buṭayn, al-Thurayyā, al-Dabarān, al-Haq‘ah, al-Han‘ah, and al-Dhirā‘; and for the season
of summer are al-Nathrah, al-Ṭarfah, al-Jabhah, al-Zubrah, al-Ṣarfah, and al-‘Awā’; and for the season of autumn are al-Ghafr, al-Zubānān,
al-Iklīl, al-Qalb, al-Shawlah, al-Na‘ā’im, and al-Baldah; and for the season of winter are Sa‘d al-Su‘ūd, Sa‘d al-Dhābiḥ, Sa‘d
al-Akhbiyah, Bulā‘, al-Farghān, al-Mu’akhkhar, and al-Rashā; and for every season seven mansions.
(Section on the saj‘ of the Arabs related to the mansions and what was transmitted from the ancients)
Some of them said: I read to our shaykh al-Kindī, may Allah تعالى have mercy on him, who said: I read to Abū Manṣūr
al-Khawlānī, who said: It has reached me from Abū Muḥammad al-Manāwī that he said: The Arabs say: when al-Sharṭayn rises, the pomegranates become even,
the branches become green, the homelands are inhabited, the neighbors exchange gifts, and the poor man spends the night in every place. And when al-Buṭayn rises,
the debt is settled. And when al-Thurayyā rises, seek for your shepherd a garment, and when it rises for him tomorrow, seek for him a waterskin. And when
al-Dabarān rises, the fires are kindled and the pools dry up. And when al-Haq‘ah rises, the people return from the darkness. And when
al-Han‘ah rises, they turn to protection. And when al-Dhirā‘ rises, the sun strips away the rays and drink is kindled in every plain.
And when al-Nathrah rises, the calf becomes weak through abundance and does not obtain in its udder a drop. And when al-Ṭarfah rises, Abū al-Ḍayf becomes easy through lightness.
And when al-‘Awā’ rises, the snakes flee and the air becomes pleasant. And when al-Simāk rises, ownership becomes abundant over wealth. And when al-Farghān
and al-Nasr rise, the pools overflow and a bridge flows. And when al-Zubānān rises, every spring is taken. And when al-Iklīl rises, the sayings become invalid.
And when al-Qalb rises, every difficulty becomes easy. And when al-Shawlah rises, the old man’s urination is delayed. And when al-Na‘ā’im rises, blessings are obtained
for every standing one. And when al-Baldah rises, then your eating is al-‘aṣīdah, and it is what comes out of butter and ghee from the bottom of the pot.
And when al-Dhābiḥ rises, the equal of the mourner is protected. And when al-Akhbiyah rises, the people love the wearing of cloaks. And when Bulā‘ rises, the
earth becomes glitter. And when al-Shawlā [~] rr al-kalā. And when al-Fargh al-Muqaddam rises, then serve and do not regret. And when al-Fargh
al-Mu’akhkhar rises, then hasten and do not delay. And when al-Sam

## Page 33

**Arabic (original):**
— ٢٧ —

اعلم وفقك الله وإياك لطاعته أن البروج الاثني عشر والمنازل الثمانية والعشرين قال تعالى فيهم:
ولقد جعلنا في السماء بروجا وزيناها للناظرين، وقال تعالى: تبارك الذي جعل في السماء بروجا
الآية، وقال تعالى: ذات البروج، وقال تعالى: قدرناه منازل، والبرج واحد البروج، والبرج
القصر، وربما يسمى البرج حصنا، قال تعالى: ولو كنتم في بروج مشيدة، وقال بعض العلماء في قوله تعالى: تبارك الذي
جعل في السماء بروجا، هي محل الكواكب السبعة السيارة وهي اثنا عشر برجا، أولها الحمل والثور
والجوزاء والسرطان والأسد والسنبلة والميزان والعقرب والقوس والجدي والدلو والحوت * فالحمل
والعقرب بيت المريخ، والثور بيت الزهرة، والجوزاء والسنبلة بيت عطارد، والسرطان بيت القمر،
والأسد بيت الشمس، والحوت بيت المشتري، والجدي والدلو بيت زحل، فهذه البروج المقسومة
على الطبائع الأربع فيكون كل واحد منها ثلاث بروج وتسمى المثلثات، فالحمل والأسد والقوس
مثلثة نارية، والثور والسنبلة والجدي مثلثة ترابية، والجوزاء والميزان والدلو مثلثة هوائية، والسرطان
والعقرب والحوت مثلثة مائية، وهذا جدول منازل القمر والبروج والشهور الرومية وهذه صورته:

| آذار | نيسان | أيار | حزيران | تموز | آب | أيلول | تشرين أول | تشرين ثاني | كانون أول | كانون ثاني | شباط |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| حمل | ثور | جوزاء | سرطان | أسد | سنبلة | ميزان | عقرب | قوس | جدي | دلو | حوت |
| مهج | ثريا | هقعة | ذراع | جبهة | صرفة | سماك | كبل | شولة | بلدة | سعود | مقدم |
| رشا | مؤخر | دبران | هنعة | طرفة | خرتان | عواء | زبانا | قلب | نعائم | بلع |

واختلف أهل التفسير في معنى البروج، فقال بعضهم هي القصور في السماء ودليله قوله: ولو كنتم في
بروج مشيدة، وقيل هي النجوم، وقيل هي السرج، وقيل أبواب السماء التي تسمى المجرة * قلت:
وقد نص ابن عباس رضي الله عنهما أنها البروج المعروفة التي أشرنا إليها اثنا عشر برجا، وأن الله تعالى
قسمها ترابيع وتثليث، وهي مقسومة على الكواكب السبعة كما ذكرنا ٣٢، وتقسيمها على
الصورة خمسة كواكب وصورته تشير مقدمه إلى جهة الغرب ومؤخره إلى جهة الشرق وهو الثور، وهي
ثلاثة والثور كواكبه والخارج من الثور أحد عشر كوكبا، وهي على صورة الوصف المقدم من الثور،
وقد نكبتا رأسه النطح، وقد قطع نصفين لمسيرته مقدمه إلى المشرق ومؤخره إلى المغرب، ومن
كواكبه الثريا والدبران وهما منازل القمر * والثالث الجوزاء ويعرف بالتوأمين ثمانية عشر كوكبا،
الخارج عن الصورة سبع كواكب، وصورته صورة توأمين قائمين أحدهما قد وضع يده على منكب
الآخر ورأسه وسائر كواكبه في الشمال والسوق على طرف المشرق وأرجلهما إلى المغرب * والرابع
السرطان وهي ستة كواكب والخارج من صورته أربعة، وهو على صورة السرطان مقدمه إلى
ناحية المشرق ومؤخره إلى المغرب والجنوب في أثر التوأمين كأنهما حملان لصورة ثمانية وصورته
قائمة ومن كواكبه الأسد كواكب نيرة * الخامس السنبلة وتعرف بالعذراء وهي ستة وعشرون

**English:**
— 27 —

Know, may God grant you success and you and obedience to Him, that the twelve signs and the twenty-eight mansions, the Exalted said concerning them: And We have certainly placed in the heaven constellations and adorned it for the beholders, and the Exalted said: Blessed is He who placed in the heaven constellations, the verse, and the Exalted said: Possessor of the constellations, and the Exalted said: We determined it as mansions, and al-burj is one of al-buruj, and al-burj is the castle, and perhaps al-burj is called a fortress, the Exalted said: And if you were in lofty towers, and some of the scholars said concerning His saying, the Exalted: Blessed is He who placed in the heaven constellations, they are the place of the seven moving stars, and they are twelve constellations, the first of them al-Hamal and al-Thawr and al-Jawza’ and al-Saratan and al-Asad and al-Sunbulah and al-Mizan and al-‘Aqrab and al-Qaws and al-Jadi and al-Dalu and al-Hut * so al-Hamal and al-‘Aqrab are the house of Mars, and al-Thawr is the house of Venus, and al-Jawza’ and al-Sunbulah are the house of Mercury, and al-Saratan is the house of the Moon, and al-Asad is the house of the Sun, and al-Hut is the house of Jupiter, and al-Jadi and al-Dalu are the house of Saturn, so these are the constellations divided upon the four natures, so each one of them is three constellations and they are called the triangles, so al-Hamal and al-Asad and al-Qaws are a fiery triangle, and al-Thawr and al-Sunbulah and al-Jadi are an earthy triangle, and al-Jawza’ and al-Mizan and al-Dalu are an airy triangle, and al-Saratan and al-‘Aqrab and al-Hut are a watery triangle, and this is a table of the lunar mansions and the constellations and the Roman months, and this is its form:

| March | April | May | June | July | August | September | October | November | December | January | February |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| Hamal | Thawr | Jawza’ | Saratan | Asad | Sunbulah | Mizan | ‘Aqrab | Qaws | Jadi | Dalu | Hut |
| Mahj | Thurayya | Haq‘ah | Dhira‘ | Jabha | Sarfah | Simak | Kabl | Shawlah | Baldah | Sa‘ud | Muqaddam |
| Rashā | Mu’akhkhar | Dabaran | Han‘ah | Tarfah | Khartan | ‘Awa’ | Zubana | Qalb | Na‘a’im | Bala‘ |

And the people of interpretation differed concerning the meaning of al-buruj, so some of them said: they are the palaces in the heaven, and its proof is His saying: And if you were in lofty towers, and it was said: they are the stars, and it was said: they are the lamps, and it was said: the gates of the heaven which are called the Milky Way * I say: Ibn ‘Abbas, may God be pleased with them both, has explicitly stated that they are the known constellations to which we have referred, twelve constellations, and that God تعالى divided them into quadrants and triads, and they are divided upon the seven planets as we mentioned 32, and their division upon the form is five planets and its form indicates its front toward the west and its rear toward the east, and it is al-Thawr, and they are three and al-Thawr, its stars, and what comes out of al-Thawr is eleven stars, and they are upon the form of the description advanced from al-Thawr, and its two horns have been struck, and it has been cut in two halves for its course, its front toward the east and its rear toward the west, and among its stars are al-Thurayya and al-Dabaran, and they are the lunar mansions * and the third is al-Jawza’ and is known as the Twins, eighteen stars, what comes out of the form is seven stars, and its form is the form of two standing twins, one of them has placed his hand upon the shoulder of the other and his head and the rest of his stars are in the north and the legs on the edge of the east and their feet toward the west * and the fourth is al-Saratan and it is six stars and what comes out of its form is four, and it is upon the form of the crab, its front toward the side of the east and its rear toward the west and the south in the track of the twins as though they were two lambs for the form of eight and its form is standing and among its stars is al-Asad, bright stars * the fifth is al-Sunbulah and is known as the Virgin and it is twenty-six

## Page 34

**Arabic (original):**
— ٢١ —

نوليا والخارج عن الصورة سبعة كواكب صورتها جارية ذات جناحين قد أسلت رأسها على
الصرفة وهي كوكب نير ومن كواكبها السماك الأعزل كوكب نير السادس الميزان ثمانية كواكب
وصورته قائمة والخارج عن الصورة تسعة ۞ السابع العقرب وهو أحد وعشرون كوكبا والخارج
عن الصورة ثلاثة وصورتها قائمة ومن كواكبه قلب العقرب وهو كوكب نير ۞ الثامن
القوس ويسمى الرامي وهو أحد وثلاثون كوكبا خلف كواكب القرب وصورته صورة حيوان
مركب من إنسان وفرس كأنه حبس ذاته إلى العنق لم يزل من مفرق العنق وصف رجل قد وضع
السهم في قوسه واعوج بالسرج ۞ العاشر الجدي وهو ثمانية وعشرون كوكبا وهو على صورة النصف
المقدم من الجدي والثاني مؤخر سمكة إلى ذنبها ۞ الحادي عشر الدالي ويعرف بالدلو وأمه هو اثنان
وأربعون كوكبا والخارج عن الصورة ثلاثة كواكب وصورته رجل قائم اليدين بإحداهما ركوة
وقد قلبها وصب الماء إلى مقدم رجليه وحدر الماء من تحتها إلى الجنوب ويسمى الدلو أيضا ۞ الثاني
عشر الحوت وهي أربعة وثلاثون كوكبا الخارج عن الصورة أربعة كواكب وصورته صورة سمكتين
قد وصل ذنب إحداهما بالأخرى جملة هذه ثلاثمائة وثلاثة وأربعون وإن الحمل أول البروج والثور
برج في السماء والجوزاء يقال إنها تعترض في السماء إلى وسطها وجوزاء كل شيء وسطه والسرطان
برج في السماء ولم يذكر الأسد والسنبلة برج في السماء وبعضهم لم يذكر الميزان والعقرب برج
في السماء وكذا القوس والجدي والدالي والحوت وقال إنها بروج في السماء والجدي نجم إلى جانب
القلب تعرف به القبلة والله أعلم ۞ فصل فيما لكل برج من البلدان ۞ اعلم أن للحمل بابل وفارس
وأذربيجان والثور همدان والأكراد ۞ والجوزاء هاجرجان وكيلان وسوفان ۞ والسرطان له
أرض الصين وشرقي خراسان ۞ والأسد له الأتراك والتتر وما والاها ۞ والميزان له أرض الروم إلى
أمريكا وقبط مصر والحبشة ۞ والعقرب له الحجاز واليمن وما يليهما والوقد له بغداد إلى أصفهان
۞ والجدي له كرمان وعمان والبحرين والهند ۞ والدالي له الكوفة إلى أرض الحجاز ۞ والحوت له
طبرستان والبحرين والموصل وإسكندرونة وهذا هو المعمور من الأرض كلها وقد ذكرنا طرفاً من
الأقاليم وما فيها بالتمام والكمال على البروج والله أعلم ۞ فصل في قسمة الزمان وهو أربعة
أقسام ۞ الأول الربيع وهو عند بعض الناس الصيف وإنما سمي الربيع ليستلان فيه الربيع وسماه
بعضهم خريفاً لأن القطر عبرت فيه ودخوله عند حلول الشمس رأس الميزان ۞ والشتاء ودخوله
عند حلول الشمس رأس الجدي ۞ والصيف ودخوله عند حلول الشمس رأس الحمل وهو عندهم
الربيع ثم الخريف ودخوله عند حلول الشمس برج السرطان وهو عندهم الصيف ۞ فصل في
الرياح وما عليها من الكلام ۞ فأولها ريح الشمال وهي التي تهب من ناحية القطب ثانيها الصبا وهي
من مطلع الشمس إذا استوى الليل والنهار وتحتها الدبور وتزعم العرب أن الدبور ترجع السحاب
وتحطه في الهواء ثم تسوقه فإذا كشف عنه استقبلته الصبا فوضعت بعضه فوق بعض حتى يصير كثيفاً
واحداً والجنوب تلحق روادفه به وتمده والشمال تمزقه الثالث الجنوب وهي التي تقابل الشمال
والدبور التي يقابلها الصبا والله أعلم ۞ فصل فيما بين كل سماء وسمائها وما ورد في ذلك من الآثار ۞
وقد

**English:**
— 21 —

Nūliyā and what comes out of the form is seven stars; its form is a maiden with two wings, she has let down her head upon al-Sarfah, and it is a bright star, and among its stars is al-Simak al-A‘zal, a bright star. The sixth, al-Mizan, eight stars, and its form is standing, and what comes out of the form is nine ۞ the seventh, al-‘Aqrab, and it is twenty-one stars, and what comes out of the form is three, and its form is standing, and among its stars is Qalb al-‘Aqrab, and it is a bright star ۞ the eighth, al-Qaws, and it is called al-Rami, and it is thirty-one stars behind the stars of al-Qurb, and its form is the form of an animal composed of a human and a horse, as though it has confined itself to the neck, not ceasing from the nape of the neck, the description of a man who has placed the arrow in his bow and is bent by the saddle ۞ the tenth, al-Jadi, and it is twenty-eight stars, and it is upon the form of the front half of al-Jadi and the second rear fish to its tail ۞ the eleventh, al-Dali, and is known as al-Dalu, and its mother is two and forty stars, and what comes out of the form is three stars, and its form is a man standing, his hands, in one of them a bucket, and he has turned it over and poured water to the front of his feet and let the water descend from beneath it to the south, and it is also called al-Dalu ۞ the twelfth, al-Hut, and they are thirty-four stars, what comes out of the form is four stars, and its form is the form of two fish, the tail of one of them having been joined to the other. The total of these is three hundred and forty-three. And al-Hamal is the first of the signs, and al-Thawr is a sign in the heaven, and al-Jawza’ it is said that it crosses in the heaven to its middle, and jawza’ of everything is its middle, and al-Saratan is a sign in the heaven, and al-Asad and al-Sunbulah were not mentioned as a sign in the heaven, and some did not mention al-Mizan and al-‘Aqrab as a sign in the heaven, and likewise al-Qaws and al-Jadi and al-Dali and al-Hut, and he said that they are signs in the heaven, and al-Jadi is a star beside the heart by which the qiblah is known, and God knows best ۞ Chapter concerning what each sign has of lands ۞ Know that for al-Hamal are Babylon and Persia and Azerbaijan, and for al-Thawr are Hamadan and the Kurds ۞ and for al-Jawza’ are Hājarjān and Gilan and Sufan ۞ and for al-Saratan is the land of China and eastern Khurasan ۞ and for al-Asad are the Turks and the Tatars and what is adjacent to them ۞ and for al-Mizan is the land of the Romans to America and the Copts of Egypt and Abyssinia ۞ and for al-‘Aqrab are the Hijaz and Yemen and what is adjacent to them, and al-Waqd is Baghdad to Isfahan ۞ and for al-Jadi are Kerman and Oman and Bahrain and India ۞ and for al-Dali is Kufa to the land of the Hijaz ۞ and for al-Hut are Tabaristan and Bahrain and Mosul and Iskandarunah, and this is the inhabited part of the whole earth, and we have mentioned a portion of the regions and what is in them in full and complete detail upon the signs, and God knows best ۞ Chapter on the division of time, and it is four divisions ۞ the first, spring, and it is with some people summer, and it was only named spring so that the spring may be made easy in it, and some named it autumn because the rain passed through it, and its entry is at the sun’s arrival at the head of Libra ۞ and winter, and its entry is at the sun’s arrival at the head of Capricorn ۞ and summer, and its entry is at the sun’s arrival at the head of Aries, and it is with them spring, then autumn, and its entry is at the sun’s arrival at the sign of Cancer, and it is with them summer ۞ Chapter on the winds and what is said concerning them ۞ so the first of them is the north wind, and it is that which blows from the side of the pole; the second is al-Saba, and it is from the rising of the sun when night and day are equal, and beneath it is al-Dabur, and the Arabs claim that al-Dabur returns the clouds and sets them down in the air, then drives them, and when al-Saba is uncovered it meets it, so it places some of it upon some until it becomes one dense mass, and the south wind joins its rear to it and extends it, and the north wind tears it apart; the third is the south wind, and it is that which faces the north, and al-Dabur, which al-Saba faces, and God knows best ۞ Chapter concerning what is between each heaven and its heaven and what has come regarding that of reports ۞ and

## Page 35

**Arabic (original):**
— ٢٩ —

وَقَدْ ذَكَرْنَا مَذْهَبَ الْأَوَائِلِ فِي صُورَةِ الْأَفْلَاكِ وَمَا يَتَعَلَّقُ بِهَا أَمَّا عَلَى مَذْهَبِ الشَّرْعِيِّينَ وَهِيَ سَمَوَاتٌ
عِنْدَهُمْ وَرُوِيَ فِي الْحَدِيثِ أَخْبَارٌ عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ وَقِيلَ الْعَبَّاسِ بْنِ عَبْدِ الْمُطَّلِبِ قَالَ كُنَّا عِنْدَ النَّبِيِّ
صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ بِالْبَطْحَاءِ فَمَرَّتْ بِنَا سَحَابَةٌ فَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ أَتَدْرُونَ مَا هَذِهِ قُلْنَا
السَّحَابُ قَالَ وَالْمُزْنُ قُلْنَا الْمُزْنُ قَالَ وَالْعَنَانُ قُلْنَا الْعَنَانُ وَسَكَتْنَا فَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ أَتَدْرُونَ
كَمْ بَيْنَ السَّمَاءِ وَالْأَرْضِ قُلْنَا اللَّهُ وَرَسُولُهُ أَعْلَمُ قَالَ بَيْنَهُمَا مَسِيرَةُ خَمْسِمِائَةِ عَامٍ وَكَسْفُ كُلِّ سَمَاءٍ أَيْ سُمْكُهَا
خَمْسُمِائَةٍ وَفَوْقَ السَّمَاءِ السَّابِعَةِ بَحْرٌ بَيْنَ أَعْلَاهُ وَأَسْفَلِهِ كَمَا بَيْنَ السَّمَاءِ وَالْأَرْضِ ثُمَّ فَوْقَهُنَّ عَالَمٌ وَأَطْلَقَهُنَّ
كَابْنِ السَّمَاءِ وَالْأَرْضِ وَاللَّهُ تَعَالَى فَوْقَ ذَلِكَ وَلَا يَخْفَى عَلَيْهِ شَيْءٌ مِنْ أَعْمَالِ بَنِي آدَمَ وَالدَّلِيلُ عَلَيْهِ قَوْلُهُ
تَعَالَى اللَّهُ الَّذِي خَلَقَ سَبْعَ سَمَوَاتٍ وَمِنَ الْأَرْضِ مِثْلَهُنَّ فَتَكُونُ مَسَافَةُ الْجَمِيعِ مَسِيرَةَ بَنِي آدَمَ هَذِهِ وَأَمَّا
الْمَلَكُ فَإِنَّهُ يَخْرِقُ الْجَمِيعَ فِي سَاعَةٍ وَاحِدَةٍ وَكَذَلِكَ الشَّيْطَانُ يَصِلُ فِي الْأَرْضِ قَبْلَ الْمَلَكِ فِي السَّمَاءِ وَرُوِيَ
أَبُو رَزِينٍ الْعُقَيْلِيُّ رَضِيَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُ سُئِلَ كَمْ بَيْنَ السَّمَاءِ وَالْأَرْضِ فَقَالَ دَعْوَةٌ مُسْتَجَابَةٌ فَقِيلَ لَهُ كَمْ بَيْنَ الْمَشْرِقِ
وَالْمَغْرِبِ قَالَ مَسِيرَةُ يَوْمٍ وَذَكَرَ الثَّعْلَبِيُّ عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ قَالَ تَطْلُعُ الشَّمْسُ كُلَّ سَنَةٍ عَلَى ثَلَاثِمِائَةٍ وَسِتِّينَ
كُوَّةً فَلَا تَطْلُعُ الْأَعْلَى كُوَّةً ذَلِكَ الْيَوْمَ إِلَى الْعَامِ الْقَابِلِ قُلْتُ وَفِي الشَّمْسِ مَنَافِعُ وَدَلَائِلُ فَمِنَ الدَّلَائِلِ أَنَّهَا
وَاحِدَةٌ وَنُورُهَا يَضِيءُ عَلَى جَمِيعِ الْآفَاقِ وَجَمِيعِ الْعَالَمِ كَذَلِكَ الْبَارِئُ سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى وَاحِدٌ وَهُوَ يُدَبِّرُ
الْعَالَمَ ثُمَّ الثَّانِي أَنَّ الشَّمْسَ مَنَافِعُهَا بَعِيدَةٌ وُصُولُهَا قَرِيبٌ وَاللَّهُ تَعَالَى بَعِيدٌ عَنِ الْخَلْقِ بِالذَّاتِ قَرِيبٌ بِالْإِجَابَةِ
الثَّالِثُ أَنَّ ضَوْءَهَا غَيْرُ مَمْنُوعٍ عَنْ أَحَدٍ فَلِذَلِكَ رِزْقُ اللَّهِ لَا يَمْنَعُ عَنْ أَحَدٍ مِنَ الْخَلْقِ وَالرَّابِعِ أَنَّ كُسُوفَهَا
دَلِيلٌ عَلَى وُجُودِ الْقِيَامَةِ وَغَيْرُهُ مِمَّا يَدُلُّ عَلَى ظُلْمَتِهَا الْخَامِسُ السَّحَابُ يُغَطِّيهَا وَلِذَلِكَ الْغَاشِيَةُ غَطَّتِ الْمَعْرِفَةَ
وَأَمَّا مَنَافِعُهَا فَكَثِيرَةٌ جِدًّا الْأُولَى أَنَّهَا سِرَاجُ الْعَالَمِ قَالَ تَعَالَى وَجَعَلْنَا الشَّمْسَ سِرَاجًا الثَّانِي أَنَّهَا النَّضَاجُ
لِأَطْعِمَتِهِمْ وَمُنْضِجَةٌ لِثَمَرَتِهِمْ وَغَيْرُ كُلْفَةٍ الثَّالِثُ أَنَّهَا تَسِيرُ مِنَ الْمَشْرِقِ إِلَى الْمَغْرِبِ لِصَلَاحِهِمُ الرَّابِعُ أَنَّهَا
لَا تَقِفُ فِي مَكَانٍ وَاحِدٍ لِئَلَّا تَضُرَّ بِالْخَلْقِ الْخَامِسُ أَنَّهَا تَكُونُ فِي الشِّتَاءِ فِي أَسْفَلِ الْبُرُوجِ وَفِي الصَّيْفِ فِي
أَعْلَاهَا لِمَنَافِعِ الْعَالَمِ السَّادِسُ أَنَّهَا لَا تَجْمَعُ مَعَ الْقَمَرِ فِي سُلْطَانِهِ لِئَلَّا يَبْطُلَ كُلُّ وَاحِدٍ مِنْهُمَا ضَوْءَ الْآخَرِ
فَإِنْ قُلْتَ هِيَ فِي الْفَلَكِ الرَّابِعِ وَلَمْ تَحْجُبْهَا السَّمَوَاتُ وَيَحْجُبُهَا الْغَيْمُ قُلْتُ السَّمَوَاتُ جَوَاهِرُ لَطِيفَةٌ شَفَّافَةٌ
وَالْغَيْمُ كَثِيفٌ لِأَنَّهُ يَتَصَاعَدُ مِنَ الْأَرْضِ وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ

( فَصْلٌ فِي الْقَمَرِ وَأَمْثَالِهِ وَمَا فِيهِ )

رُوِيَ عَنْ أَبِي هُرَيْرَةَ أَنَّهُ قَالَ النَّبِيُّ صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ هَلْ تَرَوْنَ الْبَدْرَ وَلَيْسَ دُونَهُ سَحَابٌ قَالُوا نَعَمْ
قَالَ فَهَلْ تَمَارَوْنَ فِي الشَّمْسِ لَيْسَ دُونَهَا سَحَابٌ قَالُوا نَعَمْ قَالَ صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ فَإِنَّكُمْ تَرَوْنَهُ كَذَلِكَ
فَإِنْ قُلْتَ هَلَا ضُرِبَ الْمَثَلُ بِالشَّمْسِ وَهِيَ أَضْوَأُ وَأَتَمُّ نُورًا وَالْقَمَرُ نُورُهُ مِنْهَا قُلْتُ لِأَمْرَيْنِ الْأَوَّلِ أَنَّ
نُورَ الشَّمْسِ يَغْلِبُ عَلَى الْأَبْصَارِ فَلَا يَتَمَكَّنُ مِنَ النَّظَرِ إِلَيْهِ وَالثَّانِي أَنَّهُ انْكَسَرَ لِأَجْلِ الْحَقِّ فَجَبَرَهُ اللَّهُ
تَعَالَى وَذَلِكَ لَمَّا لَطَمَ جِبْرِيلُ ضَوْءَ الْقَمَرِ بِجَنَاحِهِ انْكَسَرَ قَلْبُهُ لِأَنَّهُ كَانَ يُضَاهِي نُورَ الشَّمْسِ فَجَبَرَهُ
اللَّهُ تَعَالَى بِشَيْئَيْنِ أَحَدُهُمَا أَنَّهُ جَعَلَ الْعُيُونَ تَنْظُرُ إِلَيْهِ فِي الدُّنْيَا كُلَّ شَهْرٍ وَالثَّانِي أَنَّهُ أَمَرَ نَبِيَّهُ مُحَمَّدًا
صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ أَنْ يَضْرِبَ بِهِ الْمَثَلَ فِي أَعْظَمِ الْأَشْيَاءِ وَأَعْلَاهَا فَإِنْ قُلْتَ قَالَ اللَّهُ تَعَالَى لَا تُدْرِكُهُ
الْأَبْصَارُ وَهُوَ يُدْرِكُ الْأَبْصَارَ قُلْتُ إِنَّنَا لَا نَدَّعِي أَنَّ الْأَبْصَارَ تُدْرِكُهُ بِمَعْنَى تُحِيطُ بِهِ وَإِنَّمَا الْمُدْرِكُ قَبَسٌ

**English:**
— 29 —

And we have mentioned the doctrine of the ancients concerning the form of the spheres and what is connected to them. As for the doctrine of the legal scholars, and they are heavens with them, and there were narrated in the hadith reports from Ibn ‘Abbas, and it was said [from] al-‘Abbas ibn ‘Abd al-Muttalib: he said, We were with the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, at al-Batha’, and a cloud passed by us, and the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, said, Do you know what this is? We said, the cloud. He said, and al-muzn? We said, al-muzn. He said, and al-‘anan? We said, al-‘anan, and we were silent. Then the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, said, Do you know how much is between the heaven and the earth? We said, God and His Messenger know best. He said, Between them is the journey of five hundred years, and the thickness of each heaven, that is, its thickness, is five hundred, and above the seventh heaven is a sea, between its top and its bottom as between the heaven and the earth, then above them is a world, and He has called them, like the son of the heaven and the earth, and God تعالى is above that, and nothing of the deeds of the children of Adam is hidden from Him, and the proof for it is His saying تعالى: God is He who created seven heavens and of the earth the like of them, so the distance of the whole becomes the journey of these children of Adam, and as for the angel, he traverses the whole in one hour, and likewise the devil reaches the earth before the angel in the heaven. And it was narrated that Abu Razin al-‘Uqayli, may God تعالى be pleased with him, was asked how much is between the heaven and the earth, and he said, An answered supplication. And it was said to him, how much is between the east and the west? He said, The journey of a day. And al-Tha‘labi mentioned from Ibn ‘Abbas: he said, The sun rises every year upon three hundred and sixty windows, and the uppermost window does not rise that day until the coming year. I say: and in the sun are benefits and indications, and among the indications is that it is one and its light shines upon all the horizons and all the world; likewise the Creator, glory and exaltation be to Him, is one and He manages the world. Then the second: that the benefits of the sun are distant, its reaching is near, and God تعالى is distant from creation in essence, near in response. The third: that its light is not withheld from anyone, and for that the provision of God is not withheld from any of creation. And the fourth: that its eclipse is a proof of the existence of the Resurrection and other than it of what indicates its darkness. The fifth: the cloud covers it, and for that al-ghashiyah covered the knowledge. And as for its benefits, they are very many. The first: that it is the lamp of the world. The Exalted said: And We made the sun a lamp. The second: that it is the ripening for their foods and ripening for their fruits, and without burden. The third: that it travels from the east to the west for their welfare. The fourth: that it does not stop in one place lest it harm creation. The fifth: that it is in winter in the lowest of the signs and in summer in their highest for the benefits of the world. The sixth: that it does not gather with the moon in its authority lest each one of them nullify the light of the other. If you say: it is in the fourth sphere, and the heavens did not veil it, yet the clouds veil it, I say: the heavens are subtle, transparent substances, and the cloud is dense because it rises from the earth, and God knows best.

(Chapter on the moon and its similitudes and what is in it)

It was narrated from Abu Hurayrah that he said: The Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, said, Do you see the full moon and there is no cloud beneath it? They said, Yes. He said, Then do you dispute concerning the sun, there is no cloud beneath it? They said, Yes. He, may God bless him and grant him peace, said, Then indeed you see it likewise. If you say, Why was the example not struck with the sun, since it is brighter and more complete in light, and the moon, its light is from it? I say: for two matters. The first: that the light of the sun overpowers the eyes, so one is not able to look at it. And the second: that it was broken for the sake of the Truth, so God تعالى mended it. And that was when Gabriel struck the light of the moon with his wing, its heart was broken because it was resembling the light of the sun, so God تعالى mended it with two things: one of them is that He made the eyes look at it in the world every month, and the second is that He commanded His Prophet Muhammad, may God bless him and grant him peace, to strike by it the example in the greatest things and the highest of them. If you say, God تعالى said: The eyes do not perceive Him, while He perceives the eyes, I say: we do not claim that the eyes perceive Him in the sense of encompassing Him, but rather the perceived is a ray

---
## Page 36

**Arabic (original):**
— ٢٠ —

النَّظَرُ لِأَنَّ الْبَارِيَ سُبْحَانَهُ وَتَعَالَى يَسْتَحِيلُ عَنَّا الْوُجُودُ، وَفِي الْقَمَرِ فَوَائِدُ مِنْهَا أَنَّهُ سِرَاجٌ لِلْخَلْقِ بِاللَّيْلِ
وَمُعْجِزَةٌ لِنَبِيِّنَا صَلَّى اللَّهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ لِقَوْلِهِ تَعَالَى: اقْتَرَبَتِ السَّاعَةُ وَانْشَقَّ الْقَمَرُ، وَقَدَّرَهُ مَنَازِلَ لِنَعْرِفَ بِهَا
الْوَقْتَ، وَمَحَا مِنْ نُورِهِ تِسْعَةً وَتِسْعِينَ جُزْءًا لِقَوْلِهِ تَعَالَى: فَمَحَوْنَا آيَةَ اللَّيْلِ وَجَعَلْنَا آيَةَ النَّهَارِ مُبْصِرَةً، وَلَوْلَا
ذَلِكَ لَانْبَسَطَ النَّاسُ فِي مَعَايِشِهِمْ لَيْلًا وَنَهَارًا وَلَا كَانَ أَحَدٌ يَعْرِفُ اللَّيْلَ مِنَ النَّهَارِ، وَقَالَ أَمِيرُ الْمُؤْمِنِينَ
عَلِيُّ بْنُ أَبِي طَالِبٍ كَرَّمَ اللَّهُ وَجْهَهُ: إِنَّ النَّوْمَ تَحْتَ الْقَمَرِ عُرْيَانًا يُورِثُ الْبَرَصَ، وَإِنَّ الثَّوْبَ إِذَا غُسِلَ
وَعُلِّقَ فِي الْقَمَرِ فَإِنَّهُ يَتَهَرَّى وَيَتَغَيَّرُ لَوْنُهُ، وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ.

( فَصْلٌ: الْقَمَرُ يُؤَخَّرُ كُلَّ لَيْلَةٍ فِي مَنْزِلَةٍ مِنْهَا وَهَذِهِ أَسْمَاؤُهَا )
شَرَطَيْنِ، بَطْيْنِ، ثُرَيَّا، دَبَرَانِ، هَقْعَةٍ، هَنْعَةٍ، ذِرَاعٍ، نَثْرَةٍ، طَرْفَةٍ، جَبْهَةٍ، خُرْثَانِ، صَرْفَةٍ، عِوَا، سِمَاكٍ،
غَفْرٍ، زَبَانَا، إِكْلِيلٍ، قَلْبٍ، شَوْلَةٍ، نَعَائِمَ، بَلْدَةٍ، سُعُودٍ، سَعْدِ الذَّابِحِ، سَعْدِ بَلَعٍ، أَخْبِيَةٍ، مُقَدَّمٍ، مُؤَخَّرٍ، رِشَا.

( فَصْلٌ فِي النُّجُومِ الْأَرْبَعَةِ وَإِشَارَاتِهَا وَتَدْبِيرِهَا )
قَالَ تَعَالَى: وَهُوَ الَّذِي جَعَلَ لَكُمُ النُّجُومَ لِتَهْتَدُوا بِهَا فِي ظُلُمَاتِ الْبَرِّ وَالْبَحْرِ، وَقَالَ تَعَالَى: وَبِالنَّجْمِ هُمْ
يَهْتَدُونَ، وَرُوِيَ عَنْ سَعِيدِ بْنِ جُبَيْرٍ عَنِ ابْنِ عَبَّاسٍ رَضِيَ اللَّهُ عَنْهُ أَنَّهُ قَالَ: عِلْمُ النُّجُومِ عَالٍ نَافِعٌ عَجَزَ عَنْهُ
كَثِيرٌ مِنَ النَّاسِ، وَأَشَارَ إِلَى مَعْرِفَةِ النُّجُومِ لِأَلَى الْأَحْكَامِ الَّتِي عَلَيْهَا الْحُرُمَاتُ، وَاتَّفَقُوا عَلَى أَنَّ نُورَ الْقَمَرِ
مِنْ نُورِ الشَّمْسِ، وَاخْتَلَفُوا فِي الْكَوَاكِبِ الْمَعْرُوفَةِ وَهِيَ اثْنَانِ وَعِشْرُونَ كَوْكَبًا، فَمِنْهَا الْجَدْيُ وَهُوَ مِنَ
الدَّوَالِ عَلَى الْقِبْلَةِ، وَالْجَدْيُ نَجْمٌ إِلَى جَانِبِ الْقُطْبِ الشَّمَالِيِّ حَوْلَهُ أَنْجُمٌ صِغَارٌ ثَلَاثٌ مِنْ فَوْقٍ وَ٣ مِنْ تَحْتَ،
أَحَدُهُمَا الْفَرْقَدَانِ وَفِي الْآخَرِ نَجْمٌ يُضِيءُ يُقَابِلُهَا، وَبَيْنَهَا وَبَيْنَ ذَلِكَ أَنْجُمٌ صِغَارٌ ٣ مِنْ فَوْقٍ وَ٣ مِنْ تَحْتَ
تَدُورُ حَوْلَ الْقُطْبِ، وَالْجَدْيُ لَا تَبْرَحُ مِنْ مَكَانِهِمَا، وَإِنَّمَا يُسْتَدَلُّ بِالْجَدْيِ عَلَى الْقُطْبِ، وَالْجَدْيُ قُطْبُ هَذِهِ
الْفِرَاشَةِ، وَقِيلَ الْقُطْبُ قُطْبُهَا، وَيُسْتَدَلُّ عَلَيْهَا بِالْجَدْيِ إِذَا لَمْ يَكُنْ قَمَرٌ، فَإِذَا قَوِيَ الْقَمَرُ فَلَا يَرَاهُ إِلَّا حَدِيدُ
الْبَصَرِ، وَالسُّهَا إِلَى جَانِبِهِ وَهُوَ نَجْمٌ خَفِيٌّ يُمْتَحَنُ بِهِ النَّاسُ أَبْصَارَهُمْ، وَالْجَدْيُ الَّذِي يُعْرَفُ بِهِ الْقِبْلَةُ وَهِيَ
جَدْيُ بَنَاتِ نَعْشٍ، وَثَلَاثُ بَنَاتٍ فَنَّ الْأَرْبَعَةِ وَهَا الْمُتَقَدِّمَانِ، وَمِنَ الْبَنَاتِ الْجَدْيُ وَهُوَ
آخِرُهَا، وَالسُّهَا وَهُوَ كَوْكَبٌ خَفِيٌّ فِي بَنَاتِ نَعْشٍ، وَمِنْهُ الْمَثَلُ: أَرِهَا السُّهَى وَتَرَى الْقَمَرَ، وَكَيْفِيَّةُ مَعْرِفَةِ
الْقِبْلَةِ أَنْ تَجْعَلَهُ وَرَاءَ ظَهْرِكَ بِأَرْضِ الشَّامِ، وَفِي أَرْضِ الْعِرَاقِ مُقَابِلًا ظَهْرَ أُذُنِكَ الْيُمْنَى، وَفِي
مِصْرَ خَلْفَ الْيُسْرَى عَلَى عُلُوهَا يَكُونُ مُسْتَقْبِلًا بَابَ الْبَيْتِ إِلَى الْمَقَامِ، وَمَتَى اسْتَدْبَرْتَ الْفَرْقَدَيْنِ
أَوْ بَنَاتِ نَعْشٍ كُنْتَ مُسْتَقْبِلًا جِهَةَ الْكَعْبَةِ، وَأَمَّا الْفَرْقَدَانِ مُضِيئَانِ قَرِيبَانِ مِنَ الْقُطْبِ،
وَبَنَاتِ نَعْشٍ سَبْعَةُ كَوَاكِبَ، أَرْبَعَةٌ مِنْهَا نَعْشٌ وَثَلَاثٌ بَنَاتٌ، كَذَلِكَ بَنَاتُ نَعْشٍ الصُّغْرَى، وَالْقُطْبُ
الشَّمَالِيُّ وَالْجَنُوبِيُّ لَا يَبْلُغُهُمَا شَمْسٌ وَلَا قَمَرٌ، وَالْقُطْبُ الْجَنُوبِيُّ جَدْيُهُ يَطْلُعُ سُهَيْلٌ لَا يَظْهَرُ إِلَّا فِي جَزِيرَةِ
الْعَرَبِ، وَمِنْهَا سُهَيْلٌ إِلَى جَانِبِ الْقُطْبِ الْجَنُوبِيِّ، وَمَطَالِعُهُ مِنْ مَهَبِّ الْجَنُوبِ ثُمَّ يَصِيرُ بِجِهَةِ الْغَرْبِ
فَيَصِيرُ فِي قِبْلَةِ الْمُصَلَّى وَهُنَا يَغِيبُ، وَإِنَّ سُهَيْلًا كَوْكَبٌ مُنْفَرِدٌ عَنِ الْكَوَاكِبِ الْيَمَانِيَّةِ، وَمَطْلَعُهُ
عَلَى يَسَارِ الْقِبْلَةِ، وَيُرَى فِي جَمِيعِ أَرْضِ الْعَرَبِ مِنْ عِرَاقٍ وَشَامٍ، وَلَا يُرَى فِي بِلَادِ أَرْمِينِيَّةٍ، وَمِنْ طُلُوعِهِ
الْحِجَازُ، وَرُؤْيَتُهُ بِالْعِرَاقِ بِضْعَ عَشْرَةَ لَيْلَةً، وَقَالَ بَعْضُهُمْ: إِنَّ سُهَيْلًا نَجْمُ الْعَرَبِ، تَقُولُ: إِذَا طَلَعَ سُهَيْلٌ
لَا تَأْمَنْ مِنَ السَّيْلِ، فَاعْلَمْ أَنَّ الْكَوَاكِبَ أَلْفٌ وَاثْنَانِ وَعِشْرُونَ كَوْكَبًا، وَأَنَّهُ عُشْرُ كَوْكَبٍ فِي اثْنَيْ
عَشَرَ.

## Page 37

**Arabic (original):**
— ٣١ —
عشر صورة في طريق الشمس وهي البروج الاثنا عشر، منها ثلثمائة وستون كوكبا في إحدى
وعشرين صورة هي مائلة عن طريق الشمس إلى ناحية الشمال، منها الدب الأكبر والأصغر
والتنين وغيرهم، ومنها ثلثمائة وستة عشر كوكبا في خمسة عشر صورة مائلة عن طريق الشمس، وما
على الكواكب التي سميناها لم يقسمها عامة أرباب الصنعة أبداً، وذكرها أبو محمد عبد الجبار المعروف
في كتابه المسمى بالبصرة في الكواكب الثابتة فقال: إن الكواكب التي في الصورة
الشمالية، ومنها الدب الأصغر وهو على صورة دب واقف ماداً يديه، كواكبه سبعة وتسميه العرب
بنات نعش الصغرى، وأربعة كواكب وتسميه العرب النعش على شكل مربع والثلاثة على
ذنبه، والخارج عن الصورة منها ثمانية، من جملتها سبعة كواكب قسمها العرب بنات نعش
الصغرى الكواكب المرصودة إلى القطب الشمالي. أقول: ومنها الدب الأكبر وكواكبه سبعة وعشرون
كوكبا، والخارج عن الصورة منها ثمانية كواكب، منها سبعة كواكب تسميها العرب بنات نعش،
أربعة على بدنه وثلاثة على ذنبه، والعرب تسميها السها، ومنها التنين وهي إحدى وثلاثون كوكبا،
وصورته صورة حية كبيرة الطاقات، وابتدأوها من أربعة كواكب على شكل مربع منحرف على
رأسه سمة العقرب الفوائد، والتنين موضع في السماء ضرب من الحيات وهو أعظمها، ومنها الفكة
يقال لها الإكليل الشمالي، ويعرف في السماء ضرب من الحيات بعضه الساكنين لاستدارتها
ثمانية، وأما الفكة أيضاً فهي كواكب مستديرة خلف السماك الرامح، ومنها الجاثي على ركبتيه
صورته سبعة وعشرون كوكبا، ومنها السبعات ويقال له اللوز والصبح الرومي، ومنها السلحفاة
وكواكبها عشرة من جملتها كواكب يرسمونه النسر الواقع، والدجاجة وهي تسعة عشر كوكبا،
والخارج عن الصورة كوكبان أكبر كواكبهما في المجرة، ومنها المثلث أربع كواكب بين كوكب
السمكة وبين النسر الذي على رأس الغول، فجملة هذه الصور الشمالية ثلثمائة وستون كوكبا، وأما
الصور الجنوبية فثلثمائة واثنا عشر كوكبا، منها قيقطس اثنان وعشرون كوكبا وصورته صورة حيوان
مجرد ورجلين وذنب كذنب الحوت، ومنها الجبار ثمانية وثلاثون كوكبا وصورته رجل يجري ويده
عصا وفي وسطه منطقة وسيف، ومن كواكبه الجوزاء كوكب أحمر، ومنها اثنا عشر كوكبا مجمعة
تحت رجل الجبار شبيهة بأرنب وجهه إلى المغرب ومؤخره إلى المشرق، ومنها الكلب الأكبر ثمانية
وعشرون كوكبا والخارج عن الصورة أحد عشر خلف كواكب الجوزاء، منها السنبلة من كواكب
الشعري العبور ويسمى الأبان المرزبان، ومنها الشعري الغميصاء وهي الكواكب التي تطلع بعد
الجوزاء، وأما السرطان فالصور التي في الجوزاء والشعري الغميصاء التي في الذراع، وتزعم العرب أنهما
أختا سهيل، ومنها الإكليل الجنوبي وهو ثلاثة عشر كوكبا والخارج عن الصورة ستة كواكب،
وصورته سمكة عظيمة كواكبهما على جنوب كوكب الدالي رأسها إلى المشرق وذنبها إلى المغرب،
ومنها المجرة على جنوب حززات العة [~]، فهذه جملة من الكواكب الجنوبية والشمالية. قلت:
وهذا الذي ذكره بعضهم مقصور على الكواكب المشهورة، وأما غير المشهورة فكثيرة جداً،
فذكرها فيما بعد إن شاء الله تعالى.

**English:**
— 31 —
ten forms in the path of the sun, and they are the twelve signs of the zodiac; among them are three hundred and sixty stars in one
and twenty forms that are inclined from the path of the sun toward the north side, among them the Great Bear and the Small Bear
and the Dragon and others; and among them are three hundred and sixteen stars in fifteen forms inclined from the path of the sun, and what
we have named among the stars the generality of the masters of the craft have never divided them, and Abu Muhammad Abd al-Jabbar known
in his book called in Basra concerning the fixed stars mentioned them and said: the stars that are in the northern
form, among them the Small Bear, and it is in the form of a standing bear extending its hands, its stars are seven and the Arabs call it
the Lesser Banāt Naʿsh, and four stars and the Arabs call it the bier in the form of a square and the three upon
its tail, and what is outside the form of them is eight, among them seven stars the Arabs divided [as] Banāt Naʿsh
al-Sughrā, the observed stars toward the north pole. I say: and among them is the Great Bear and its stars are twenty-seven
stars, and what is outside the form of them is eight stars, among them seven stars the Arabs call Banāt Naʿsh,
four upon its body and three upon its tail, and the Arabs call them al-suhā, and among them is the Dragon, and it is thirty-one stars,
and its form is the form of a great serpent of many coils, and they began it from four stars in the form of an inclined square upon
its head the mark of the scorpion, the benefits, and the Dragon is placed in the heaven, a kind of serpents, and it is the greatest of them; and among them is al-fakka
called the Northern Crown, and there is known in the heaven a kind of serpents, some of them the dwellers because of its roundness,
eight; and as for al-fakka also, it is stars round behind al-simāk al-rāmih; and among them is the one kneeling upon his knees,
its form is twenty-seven stars; and among them are the seven and it is said to it al-lūz and al-subḥ al-rūmī; and among them is the tortoise
and its stars are ten, among them stars they draw as al-nasr al-wāqiʿ, and the hen, and it is nineteen stars,
and what is outside the form are two stars, the larger of whose stars are in the Milky Way; and among them is the triangle, four stars between the star
of the fish and the eagle that is upon the head of al-ghūl; so the total of these northern forms is three hundred and sixty stars. As for
the southern forms, they are three hundred and twelve stars, among them Cetus, twenty-two stars, and its form is the form of an animal
bare and two legs and a tail like the tail of the whale; and among them is Orion, thirty-eight stars, and its form is a man running and his hand
a staff and in his middle a belt and a sword, and among his stars is al-jawzāʾ, a red star; and among them are twelve stars gathered
under the leg of Orion, resembling a rabbit whose face is toward the west and its rear toward the east; and among them is the Great Dog, eight
and twenty stars, and what is outside the form is eleven behind the stars of al-jawzāʾ, among them al-sunbulah from the stars of
al-shiʿrā al-ʿabūr and it is called al-abbān al-marzbān; and among them is al-shiʿrā al-ghumaysāʾ, and they are the stars that rise after
al-jawzāʾ; as for Cancer, the forms that are in al-jawzāʾ and al-shiʿrā al-ghumaysāʾ that are in the arm, and the Arabs claim that they are
the two sisters of Suhayl; and among them is the Southern Crown, and it is thirteen stars, and what is outside the form is six stars,
and its form is a great fish, their stars upon the south of the star al-dālī, its head toward the east and its tail toward the west,
and among them is the Milky Way upon the south of ḥazzāt al-ʿa [~], so this is the total of the southern and northern stars. I say:
and this which some of them mentioned is restricted to the famous stars, and as for the غير famous, they are very many,
so mention of them will come later, if God تعالى wills.

## Page 38

**Arabic (original):**
— ٣٢ —

{ فَصْلٌ فِي أَجْرَامِهَا وَمَا يَتَعَلَّقُ بِهَا }
اعْلَمْ أَنَّ جِرْمَ الشَّمْسِ قَدْرُ الدُّنْيَا مِائَةٍ وَنِصْفٍ وَجِرْمُ الْقَمَرِ قَدْرُ الدُّنْيَا تِسْعَةٍ وَثَلَاثِينَ مَرَّةً
وَكَذَلِكَ الزُّهَرَةُ وَعُطَارِدُ وَالْمِرِّيخُ وَجِرْمُ الْمُشْتَرِي قَدْرُ الدُّنْيَا اثْنَيْنِ وَثَمَانِينَ مَرَّةً وَجِرْمُ زُحَلَ قَدْرُ الدُّنْيَا
تِسْعَةٍ وَتِسْعِينَ مَرَّةً وَبَعْضُ عُلَمَائِنَا قَالَ أَنَّ جِرْمَ الشَّمْسِ خَمْسَةَ عَشَرَ دَرَجَةً أَمَامَهَا وَكَذَا خَلْفَهَا وَجِرْمُ
الْقَمَرِ اثْنَا عَشَرَ دَرَجَةً أَمَامَهُ وَكَذَا خَلْفَهُ وَجِرْمُ الْمِرِّيخِ ثَمَانِ دَرَجَاتٍ خَلْفَهُ وَكَذَا أَمَامَهُ وَجِرْمُ الزُّهَرَةِ
سَبْعُ دَرَجَاتٍ أَمَامَهَا وَكَذَا خَلْفَهَا وَجِرْمُ عُطَارِدَ كَذَلِكَ وَاللَّهُ تَعَالَى أَعْلَمُ
{ فَصْلٌ أَنَّ كُلَّ كَوْكَبٍ فِي السَّمَاءِ مِقْدَارُ الدُّنْيَا مِرَارًا } وَأَمَّا الْكَوَاكِبُ الثَّابِتَةُ وَهُمْ خَمْسَةَ
عَشَرَ كَوْكَبًا الشِّعْرَى الْعَبُورُ وَالسَّمَاكُ وَالنَّسْرُ الطَّائِرُ وَقَلْبُ الْأَسَدِ وَنَحْوُهَا كُلُّ كَوْكَبٍ مِنْهَا قَدْرُ
الدُّنْيَا أَرْبَعَةٍ وَسِتُّونَ مَرَّةً

{ فَصْلٌ فِي قَطْعِ النُّجُومِ السَّبْعَةِ الْأَفْلَاكَ }
اعْلَمْ أَنَّ الْقَمَرَ يَقْطَعُ الْفَلَكَ فِي تِسْعَةٍ وَعِشْرِينَ يَوْمًا وَثُلُثِ يَوْمٍ وَعُطَارِدُ يَقْطَعُهُ فِي ثَمَانِيَةٍ وَعِشْرِينَ يَوْمًا
وَالزُّهَرَةُ تَقْطَعُهُ فِي مِائَتَيْنِ وَأَرْبَعَةٍ وَعِشْرِينَ يَوْمًا وَرُبْعِ يَوْمٍ وَالشَّمْسُ تَقْطَعُهُ فِي ثَلَاثِمِائَةٍ وَخَمْسَةٍ وَسِتِّينَ
يَوْمًا وَرُبْعِ يَوْمٍ وَالْمِرِّيخُ يَقْطَعُهُ فِي سِتِّمِائَةٍ وَثَلَاثِينَ وَالْمُشْتَرِي يَقْطَعُهُ فِي إِحْدَى عَشْرَةَ سَنَةً وَزُحَلُ يَقْطَعُهُ
فِي تِسْعَةٍ وَعِشْرِينَ سَنَةً وَاللَّهُ أَعْلَمُ

{ فَصْلٌ فِي مَقَامَاتِ الْبُرُوجِ }
اعْلَمْ أَنَّ مَقَامَ الْقَمَرِ فِي كُلِّ بُرْجٍ يَوْمَانِ وَثَلَاثُ لَيَالٍ وَمَقَامَ عُطَارِدَ فِي كُلِّ بُرْجٍ خَمْسَةَ عَشَرَ يَوْمًا وَمَقَامَ
الزُّهَرَةِ فِي كُلِّ بُرْجٍ خَمْسَةٍ وَعِشْرِينَ يَوْمًا وَمَقَامَ الْمُشْتَرِي فِي كُلِّ بُرْجٍ سَنَةً وَمَقَامَ زُحَلَ فِي كُلِّ
بُرْجٍ ثَلَاثُونَ شَهْرًا

{ فَصْلٌ فِي شَرَفِ الْكَوْكَبِ }
اعْلَمْ أَنَّ شَرَفَ الْقَمَرِ الثَّوْرُ وَشَرَفَ عُطَارِدَ السُّنْبُلَةُ وَشَرَفَ الزُّهَرَةِ الْحُوتُ وَشَرَفَ الشَّمْسِ الْحَمَلُ
وَشَرَفَ الْمِرِّيخِ الْجَدْيُ وَشَرَفَ الْمُشْتَرِي السَّرَطَانُ وَزُحَلَ الْمِيزَانُ وَالثُّرَيَّا قِيلَ هِيَ سِرَاجُ الْعَالَمِ فِي
السَّمَاءِ لِأَنَّهَا تَجَمُّعُ النُّجُومِ كَالْعِقْدَةِ وَهِيَ بِالسَّمَاءِ وَتُسَمِّيهَا الْعَرَبُ النُّجُومَ لِأَنَّهُ لَيْسَ فِي السَّمَاءِ بَقْعَةٌ
أَكْثَرُ نُجُومًا مِنْهَا

{ فَصْلٌ فِيمَا لِكُلِّ كَوْكَبٍ مِنَ الْأَيَّامِ السَّبْعِ } الْأَحَدُ لِلشَّمْسِ وَالِاثْنَيْنِ لِلْقَمَرِ وَالثُّلَاثَاءِ لِلْمِرِّيخِ
وَالْأَرْبِعَاءِ لِعُطَارِدَ وَالْخَمِيسِ لِلْمُشْتَرِي وَالْجُمُعَةِ لِلزُّهَرَةِ وَالسَّبْتِ لِزُحَلَ
{ فَصْلٌ فِي اقْتِرَانِ الْكَوَاكِبِ } وَمَعْنَى الِاقْتِرَانِ أَنْ يَكُونَ الْكَوْكَبُ فِي بُرْجٍ وَالْآخَرُ فِي
نَظِيرِهِ وَالِاجْتِمَاعِ أَنْ يَجْتَمِعَا فِي بُرْجٍ وَاحِدٍ فَيُؤَثِّرُ بِأَمْرِ اللَّهِ تَعَالَى كُلَّ فِعْلٍ ذَلِكَ فِي الْقُرْآنِ فَافْهَمْ ذَلِكَ فَإِذَا
قَارَبَ زُحَلُ الْمُشْتَرِي عَمَّتِ الْحُرُوبُ فِي الْأَقَالِيمِ وَيَمُوتُ مَلِكٌ مِنْ مُلُوكِ الدُّنْيَا وَالْأَرْضِ وَإِذَا قَارَبَ الْمِرِّيخُ
زُحَلَ كَانَ ذَلِكَ وَإِذَا قَارَبَ زُحَلُ الشَّمْسَ كَانَ ذَلِكَ وَإِذَا قَارَنَ زُحَلُ الزُّهَرَةَ دَلَّ عَلَى غَلَاءِ الْأَسْعَارِ
وَالْقَحْطِ وَإِذَا قَارَنَ زُحَلُ عُطَارِدَ دَلَّ عَلَى صَلَاحِ حَالِ الْكِتَابِ وَإِذَا قَارَنَ الْقَمَرُ دَلَّ عَلَى ظُهُورِ الْجُورِ
فِي الْأَحْكَامِ وَإِذَا قَارَنَ الْمُشْتَرِي الْمِرِّيخَ فِي الْعَالَمِ شَدَائِدُ كَثِيرَةً

{ فَصْلٌ }

**English:**
— 32 —

{ Chapter on their bodies and what is connected to them }
Know that the body of the sun is the measure of the world one hundred and a half, and the body of the moon is the measure of the world thirty-nine times,
and likewise Venus and Mercury and Mars, and the body of Jupiter is the measure of the world eighty-two times, and the body of Saturn is the measure of the world
ninety-nine times, and some of our scholars said that the body of the sun is fifteen degrees in front of it and likewise behind it, and the body of
the moon is twelve degrees in front of it and likewise behind it, and the body of Mars is eight degrees behind it and likewise in front of it, and the body of Venus
seven degrees in front of it and likewise behind it, and the body of Mercury likewise, and God Most High knows.
{ Chapter that every star in the heaven is the measure of the world many times } And as for the fixed stars, and they are fifteen
stars, al-shiʿrā al-ʿabūr and al-simāk and al-nasr al-ṭāʾir and the heart of the lion and the like of them, every star among them is the measure of
the world sixty-four times.

{ Chapter on the traversing of the seven stars the spheres }
Know that the moon traverses the sphere in twenty-nine days and a third of a day, and Mercury traverses it in twenty-eight days,
and Venus traverses it in two hundred and twenty-four days and a quarter of a day, and the sun traverses it in three hundred and sixty-five
days and a quarter of a day, and Mars traverses it in six hundred and thirty, and Jupiter traverses it in eleven years, and Saturn traverses it
in twenty-nine years, and God knows.

{ Chapter on the stations of the signs }
Know that the station of the moon in every sign is two days and three nights, and the station of Mercury in every sign is fifteen days, and the station of
Venus in every sign is twenty-five days, and the station of Jupiter in every sign is a year, and the station of Saturn in every
sign is thirty months.

{ Chapter on the dignity of the star }
Know that the dignity of the moon is Taurus, and the dignity of Mercury is Virgo, and the dignity of Venus is Pisces, and the dignity of the sun is Aries,
and the dignity of Mars is Capricorn, and the dignity of Jupiter is Cancer, and Saturn is Libra, and the Pleiades it was said are the lamp of the world in
the heaven because they are the gathering of the stars like the knot, and it is in the heaven, and the Arabs call it the stars because there is not in the heaven a spot
more starry than it.

{ Chapter on what belongs to each star of the seven days } Sunday is for the sun, and Monday for the moon, and Tuesday for Mars,
and Wednesday for Mercury, and Thursday for Jupiter, and Friday for Venus, and Saturday for Saturn.
{ Chapter on the conjunction of the stars } And the meaning of conjunction is that the star be in a sign and the other in
its opposite, and of meeting is that they meet in one sign, so it affects by the command of God Most High every act; that in the Qurʾan, so understand that. If
Saturn approaches Jupiter, wars become general in the regions, and a king from the kings of the world and the earth dies; and if Mars approaches
Saturn, that is; and if Saturn approaches the sun, that is; and if Saturn conjoins Venus, it indicates the highness of prices
and drought; and if Saturn conjoins Mercury, it indicates the rectification of the state of the book; and if the moon conjoins, it indicates the ظهور of injustice
in judgments; and if Jupiter conjoins Mars, in the world are many hardships.

{ Chapter }

## Page 39

**Arabic (original):**
- ٣٣ -

( فصل في طبائع الكواكب ) اعلم أن القمر بارد مؤنث يلغي بلغمي فيه حرارة عرضية لأن ضوءه
من ضوء الشمس وسلطانه الضحك والزينة وهو مرة صفراء . عطارد يذكر ويؤنث وهو
مرة سعد ومرة نحس ويستوي فيه طبيعة الحرارة وسلطانه المنطق والكتابة . الزهرة أنثى وهي سعد
باردة رطبة لها البلغم وسلطانها الزواج والفرح ولها الشهوة ونظم الإكليل وتكلف وتأليف
القلوب والنساء والحسن واللهو والضحك . الشمس مذكرة حارة يابسة لها المرة الصفراء وهي
سعد بالنظر نحس بالمقابلة جوهرها الذهب وسلطانها على العلوم ولها الشرف الأسمى والفرح
والسرور والملك . والمريخ مؤنث حار يابس له المرة الصفراء وجوهره الحديد ومذاقته مرة وسلطانه
على الرأس والمعدة والقتل والنساء وغيرهم . المشتري مذكر معتدل روحاني سعد له الدم
وجوهره القصدير ومذاقته ولونه أبيض وسلطانه على الريح الساكن في القلب وله العطايا الجزيلة
والرياسة . زحل مذكر بارد يابس مظلم له المرة السوداء وجوهره الرصاص ومذاقته مرة ولونه
أسود وسلطانه على المذاكرة وله الحرارة والجور والقهر والجبرة . قلت وزعم قوم أن هذه
البروج والأفلاك والكواكب السيارة تفعل في العالم التأثير وهي مدبرة العوالم واحتجوا لذلك بقوله
تعالى فالمذبرات أمرا ونحو ذلك * ونحن لا فقد ورد أن النبي صلى الله عليه وسلم لما صعد إلى
السماء أخبرنا بالبروج والنجوم وغيرها فما جاء عنه في هذا الباب فمقبول ومنهي عنه فلا يلتفت إليه
بل قامت البراهين والدلائل على أن الباري تعالى اخترعها وأنشأها وأما قوله تعالى فالمذبرات
أمرا فقد قال ابن عباس إن الملائكة بعضهم موكل بالأرزاق وبعضهم موكل بالمطر وبعضهم موكل
بالرياح لما ذكر في خلق الملائكة ولو كانت مدبرات فبإذن الباري جل وعلا لأنه القادر العليم
الحكيم فسبحان من هذه القدرة قدرته والحكمة حكمته ألا له الخلق والأمر تبارك الله رب العالمين
والله أعلم بالصواب

( الباب الخامس في أسرار البسملة وما لها من الخواص والبركات الخفيات )
اعلم وفقني الله وإياك لطاعته وفهم أسرار أسمائه أن من علم ما أودع الله تعالى في بسم الله الرحمن
الرحيم من الأسرار لم يحترق بالنار ولم تأكله ومن كتبها ووفقها لم يحترق بالنار وقد اتفق جميع العلماء
على أنه يستحب الابتداء ببسم الله الرحمن الرحيم في كل أمر ذي بال اتباعا للكتاب العزيز ولما روى
أبو هريرة رضي الله تعالى عنه عن النبي صلى الله عليه وسلم أنه قال كل أمر ذي بال لا يبدأ فيه ببسم الله
الرحمن الرحيم فهو أجذم وفي رواية فهو أقطع وفي رواية فهو أبتر ومعناه قليل البركة ولا يبارك فيه
وروى أن الكتب المنزلة من السماء إلى الأرض مائة وأربعة . صحف شيث ستون . وصحف إبراهيم
ثلاثون . وصحف موسى قبل التوراة عشر . والتوراة . والإنجيل . والزبور . والفرقان ومعاني كل
الكتب مجموعة في الفرقان ومعاني الفرقان في الفاتحة ومعاني الفاتحة مجموعة في البسملة ومعاني
البسملة مجموعة في بائها ومعناها أن كل ما كون يكون ما يكون وروى أن بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم لما نزلت
اهتز العرش لنزولها وقالت الخزنة لم يدخل النار من قرأها وهي تسعة عشر حرفا على عدد
الملائكة الموكلين بالنار عافانا الله منها وروى عن جابر رضي الله تعالى عنه أنه قال لما نزلت

- ٣ - شمس المعارف - أول )

**English:**
- 33 -

(Chapter on the natures of the planets) Know that the moon is cold, feminine, phlegmatic; in it is an accidental heat because its light
is from the light of the sun, and its dominion is laughter and adornment, and it is yellow bile. Mercury is masculine and feminine, and it is
sometimes fortunate and sometimes unfortunate, and the nature of heat is equal in it, and its dominion is speech and writing. Venus is female and she is fortunate,
cold, moist; she has phlegm, and her dominion is marriage and joy, and she has desire, and the arranging of the crown, and affectation, and the bringing together
of hearts and women and beauty and amusement and laughter. The sun is masculine, hot, dry; it has yellow bile, and it is
fortunate by aspect, unfortunate by opposition; its essence is gold, and its dominion is over the sciences, and it has the highest nobility and joy
and gladness and kingship. And Mars is feminine, hot, dry; it has yellow bile, and its essence is iron, and its taste is bitter, and its dominion is
over the head and the stomach and killing and women and others. Jupiter is masculine, moderate, spiritual, fortunate; it has blood
and its essence is tin, and its taste and its color are white, and its dominion is over the wind dwelling in the heart, and it has abundant gifts
and leadership. Saturn is masculine, cold, dry, dark; it has black bile, and its essence is lead, and its taste is bitter, and its color is
black, and its dominion is over deliberation, and it has heat and injustice and subjugation and coercion. I say: and a people claimed that these
zodiacal signs and spheres and moving planets act in the world by influence, and that they are the governors of the worlds, and they argued for that by His
saying, تعالى, “and the directors of an affair” and the like * and we do not, for it has been related that the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, when he ascended to
the heaven informed us of the zodiacal signs and the stars and others, so whatever has come from him in this الباب is accepted, and whatever is forbidden from it is not to be regarded;
rather, the proofs and evidences have been established that the Creator, تعالى, invented them and brought them into being. As for His saying, تعالى, “and the directors
of an affair,” Ibn Abbas said that the angels, some of them are entrusted with provisions, and some of them are entrusted with rain, and some of them are entrusted
with the winds, as mentioned in the creation of the angels. And if they were directors, then by the permission of the Creator, جل وعلا, because He is the Powerful, the Knowing,
the Wise. So glory be to Him whose power is His power and whose wisdom is His wisdom; surely to Him belongs the خلق and the الأمر. Blessed is God, Lord of the worlds,
and God knows best what is right.

(The fifth chapter on the secrets of the basmalah and what there is of its hidden properties and blessings)
Know, may God grant me and you success in obedience to Him and understanding the secrets of His names, that whoever knows what God تعالى has deposited in Bism Allah al-Rahman
al-Rahim of secrets will not be burned by fire and it will not consume him, and whoever writes it and makes it successful will not be burned by fire. And all the scholars have agreed
that it is recommended to begin with Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim in every matter of importance, following the Noble Book and what Abu Hurayrah, may God تعالى be pleased with him, narrated
from the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, that he said: every matter of importance in which Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim is not begun is cut off, and in a narration it is cut off, and in a narration it is mutilated; and its meaning is little blessing and there is no blessing in it.
And it is narrated that the books sent down from heaven to earth are one hundred and four: the صحف of Sheth sixty, and the صحف of Ibrahim
thirty, and the صحف of Moses before the Torah ten, and the Torah, and the Gospel, and the Psalms, and the Furqan. And the meanings of all
the books are gathered in the Furqan, and the meanings of the Furqan are in al-Fatiha, and the meanings of al-Fatiha are gathered in the basmalah, and the meanings
of the basmalah are gathered in its ba’, and its meaning is that everything that is formed will be what will be. And it is narrated that when Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim was revealed
the Throne trembled at its descent, and the treasurers said: no one who recites it will enter the Fire, and it is nineteen letters according to the number of
the angels entrusted with the Fire—may God preserve us from it. And it is narrated from Jabir, may God تعالى be pleased with him, that he said: when it was revealed

- ٣ - شمس المعارف - أول )

## Page 40

**Arabic (original):**

بِسْمِ اللهِ الرَّحْمَنِ الرَّحِيمِ هَرَبَ الْغَيْمُ إِلَى الْمَشْرِقِ وَسَكَنَتْ الرِّيَاحُ وَهَاجَتِ الْبِحَارُ وَأَصْغَتْ لَهَا بِإِذْنِ
اللهِ وَرُجِمَتْ الشَّيَاطِينُ مِنَ السَّمَاءِ وَأَقْسَمَ رَبُّ الْعِزَّةِ لَا يُسَمَّى اسْمِي عَلَى مَرِيضِ الْأَشْوَاقِ وَلَا عَلَى شَيْءٍ
لَا بُدَّ لَهُ فِيهِ وَقَالَ ابْنُ مَسْعُودٍ رَضِيَ اللهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُ مَنْ أَرَادَ أَنْ يُنْجِيَهُ اللهُ مِنَ الزَّانِيَةِ التِّسْعَةَ عَشَرَ
فَلْيَكْتُبْهَا وَهِيَ تِسْعَةَ عَشَرَ حَرْفًا كُلَّ حَرْفٍ نَجَاةٌ مِنْ كُلِّ وَاحِدٍ مِنْهُمْ وَمَنْ أَكْثَرَ مِنْ ذِكْرِهَا رَزَقَ
الْهَيْبَةَ عِنْدَ الْعَالَمِ الْعُلْوِيِّ وَالسُّفْلِيِّ وَبِهَا قَامَ مُلْكُ سُلَيْمَانَ بْنَ دَاوُدَ عَلَيْهِمَا السَّلَامُ وَمَنْ كَتَبَهَا مِائَةَ مَرَّةٍ وَحَمَلَهَا
رُزِقَ الْهَيْبَةَ فِي الْقُلُوبِ وَرُوِيَ عَنِ عَبْدِ اللهِ بْنِ عُمَرَ رَضِيَ اللهُ تَعَالَى عَنْهُ أَنَّهُ قَالَ مَنْ كَانَتْ لَهُ حَاجَةٌ
إِلَى اللهِ تَعَالَى فَلْيَصُمِ الْأَرْبِعَاءَ وَالْخَمِيسَ وَالْجُمُعَةَ فَإِذَا كَانَ يَوْمُ الْجُمُعَةِ اغْتَسَلَ وَذَهَبَ إِلَى الْجَامِعِ وَتَصَدَّقَ
بِشَيْءٍ فَإِذَا صَلَّى الْجُمُعَةَ قَالَ بَعْدَهَا اللَّهُمَّ إِنِّي أَسْأَلُكَ بِاسْمِكَ الرَّحْمَنِ الرَّحِيمِ اللهِ لَا إِلَهَ إِلَّا هُوَ الْحَيُّ الْقَيُّومُ إِلَى
آخِرِ الْآيَةِ الَّذِي عَنَتْ لَهُ الْوُجُوهُ وَخَشَعَتْ لَهُ الْأَصْوَاتُ وَوَجِلَتِ الْقُلُوبُ مِنْ خَشْيَتِهِ أَسْأَلُكَ أَنْ تُصَلِّيَ
وَتُسَلِّمَ عَلَى سَيِّدِنَا مُحَمَّدٍ وَعَلَى آلِهِ وَصَحْبِهِ وَسَلَّمَ وَأَنْ تَقْضِيَ حَاجَتِي هَكَذَا وَهِيَ كَذَا وَكَذَا فَيُسَمِّيهَا وَكَانَ يَقُولُ
لَا تَعْلَمُوهَا سُفَهَاءَكُمْ فَيَدْعُو بَعْضُهُمْ عَلَى بَعْضٍ فَيُسْتَجَابُ لَهُمْ فِي الْوَقْتِ وَرُوِيَ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ صَلَّى اللهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ
أَنَّهُ قَالَ مَا بَيْنَ بِسْمِ اللهِ الرَّحْمَنِ الرَّحِيمِ وَبَيْنَ اسْمِ اللهِ الْأَعْظَمِ إِلَّا كَمَا بَيْنَ بَيَاضِ الْعَيْنِ وَسَوَادِهَا وَقَالَ
النَّبِيُّ صَلَّى اللهُ عَلَيْهِ وَسَلَّمَ مَا بَيْنَ الْآدَمِيِّينَ وَالشَّيَاطِينِ إِلَّا بِسْمِ اللهِ الرَّحْمَنِ الرَّحِيمِ فَهُوَ هُوَ الِاسْمُ الْمُضْمَرُ
الَّذِي يَدُلُّ عَلَى أَنَّ مَا بَعْدَهُ الِاسْمُ الْأَعْظَمُ وَهُوَ اللهُ لِأَنَّ الِاسْمَ الْأَعْظَمَ هُوَ الْجَلَالَةُ وَهُوَ قُطْبُ الْأَسْمَاءِ وَإِلَيْهِ
تَرْجِعُ وَهُوَ فِي الْأَسْمَاءِ كَالْعَلَمِ لِأَنَّكَ إِذَا سَأَلْتَ مِنَ الرَّحْمَنِ فَتَقُولُ اللهُ وَكَذَا سَائِرُ الْأَسْمَاءِ تُضَافُ إِلَيْهِ
وَتُعْرَفُ بِالْجَلَالَةِ وَعُلُوِّ رِفْعَتِهِ وَلَهُ شَرَفٌ زَائِدٌ عَلَى الْأَسْمَاءِ وَهُوَ أَنَّكَ إِذَا أَزَلْتَ مِنْهُ حَرْفَ الْأَلِفِ
بَقِيَ لِلَّهِ وَإِذَا أَزَلْتَ مِنْهُ اللَّامَ الْأُولَى بَقِيَ لَهُ وَإِذَا أَزَلْتَ الثَّانِيَةَ أَيْضًا بَقِيَ هُوَ فَكُلُّ حَرْفٍ
مِنْهُ قَائِمٌ بِذَاتِهِ وَلَيْسَ كَذَلِكَ غَيْرُهُ مِنَ الْأَسْمَاءِ لِأَنَّكَ إِذَا أَزَلْتَ مِنْهَا حَرْفًا بَطَلَ مَعْنَاهُ وَهَذَا الِاسْمُ
الْأَعْظَمُ حُرُوفُهُ لَمْ تُخْتَلْ فَإِنَّهُ شَرَفٌ عَلَى الْأَسْمَاءِ وَدَلِيلٌ عَلَى أَنَّهُ الذَّاتُ الْمُكَرَّمَةُ الثَّابِتَةُ الْعِزِّ وَالْبَقَاءِ وَلَهُ
شَرَفٌ آخَرُ يَدُلُّ عَلَى الذَّاتِ الْوَحْدَانِيَّةِ الرُّبُوبِيَّةِ وَيَدُلُّ عَلَى تَوْحِيدِ الْإِلَهِيَّةِ فَأَنَّ أَوَّلَهُ الْأَلِفُ وَهُوَ أَوَّلُ
الْحُرُوفِ وَأَوَّلُ الْأَعْدَادِ وَآخِرُهُ حَرْفُ الْهَاءِ وَهُوَ يُشِيرُ إِلَى تَوْحِيدِ الْأُلُوهِيَّةِ وَهُوَ لَا يُوجَدُ فِي غَيْرِهِ مِنَ
الْأَسْمَاءِ وَهُوَ يَقُولُ بِسْمِ حَالِهِ أَنَا الْأَوَّلُ وَالْآخِرُ وَالظَّاهِرُ وَالْبَاطِنُ ثُمَّ أَعْقَبَهُ بِصِفَةِ الرَّحْمَانِيَّةِ قَالَ تَعَالَى
قُلِ ادْعُوا اللهَ أَوِ ادْعُوا الرَّحْمَنَ أَيَّمَا تَدْعُوا فَخَيَّرَكَ بَيْنَ أَنْ تَقُولَ يَا اللهُ أَوْ يَا رَحْمَنُ فَإِنَّهُ جَامِعُ الصِّفَتَيْنِ
الرَّحْمَانِيَّتَيْنِ وَالْكَلَامُ الْكَرِيمُ فَإِنْ شِئْتَ طَلَبَ الرَّحْمَةِ فَقُلْ يَا رَحْمَنُ وَهُوَ الْأَخَصُّ لِأَنَّ اللهَ تَعَالَى هُوَ أَخَصُّ
الْأَسْمَاءِ وَأَعْظَمُهُمْ اتِّفَاقًا فَهُوَ اسْمٌ رَبَّانِيٌّ وَأَمَّا تَفْسِيرُهُ فَهُوَ أَنْ يُخْرِجَ الْأَشْيَاءَ مِنَ الْعَدَمِ إِلَى الْوُجُودِ وَلَهُ مَعَانٍ
أُخَرُ يَجِبُ عَلَى النَّاظِرِ فِيهَا كَفُّهَا عَنِ السُّفَهَاءِ لِئَلَّا يَتَوَصَّلُوا إِلَى فِعْلِ الْمُنْكَرَاتِ وَالْمُحَرَّمَاتِ فَيَسْقُطَ
عِنْدَ اللهِ مِثْلَ بَاعَ وَلَمَا أَرَادَ اللهُ تَعَالَى بِهِ مَعْصِيَةً نَعُوذُ بِاللهِ تَعَالَى مِنْ غَضَبِهِ اللَّهُمَّ لَا تَجْعَلْنَا مِمَّنْ يَسْتَعِينُ
بِأَسْمَائِكَ عَلَى مَعَاصِيهِ وَهَذَا الِاسْمُ لَهُ حُرُوفٌ أَرْبَعَةٌ أَلِفٌ وَلَامٌ وَهَاءٌ لَهَا الطَّبَائِعُ أَرْبَعَةٌ وَالْأَقْطَارُ
أَرْبَعَةٌ شَرْقٌ وَغَرْبٌ وَشَمَالٌ وَجَنُوبٌ وَمَلَائِكَةُ التَّسْبِيحِ أَرْبَعَةٌ جِبْرِيلُ وَمِيكَائِيلُ وَإِسْرَافِيلُ وَعِزْرَائِيلُ
فَجِبْرِيلُ صَاحِبُ الرِّسَالَةِ إِلَى الْمُرْسَلِينَ وَصَاحِبُ الْغَلَبَةِ وَالْقَهْرِ وَبِهِ أَهْلَكَ اللهُ تَعَالَى الْكَفَرَةَ مِنَ الْأُمَمِ
. الْقِذَة

**English:**
Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim, the clouds fled to the east, and the winds became still, and the seas raged, and they listened to it by the permission of
God, and the devils were pelted from the heaven, and the Lord of Might swore: My name is not named upon the sick of longings, nor upon a thing
in which there is no escape for it. And Ibn Mas‘ud, may God تعالى be pleased with him, said: whoever wishes that God save him from the nineteen adulteresses,
let him write it, and it is nineteen letters; every letter is salvation from every one of them. And whoever increases in its remembrance is provided with
awe among the upper world and the lower world, and by it the kingdom of Sulayman ibn Dawud, upon them both السلام, was established. And whoever writes it one hundred times and carries it
is provided with awe in the hearts. And it is narrated from Abdullah ibn Umar, may God تعالى be pleased with him, that he said: whoever has a need
to God تعالى, let him fast Wednesday and Thursday and Friday; then when it is Friday, let him bathe and go to the congregational mosque and give charity
with something; then when he prays the Friday prayer, let him say after it: O God, I ask You by Your name, al-Rahman al-Rahim, God, there is no god except Him, the Living, the Self-Subsisting, to
the end of the verse, “to whom the faces are humbled, and the voices are subdued, and the hearts are terrified from His fear”; I ask You to send blessings
and peace upon our master Muhammad and upon his family and his companions, and peace, and that You fulfill my need thus and it is thus and thus, so he names it. And he used to say:
do not let your fools know it, lest some of them invoke against some of them, and it be answered for them at the time. And it is narrated from the Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace,
that he said: there is nothing between Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim and between the Greatest Name of God except as between the whiteness of the eye and its blackness. And the
Prophet, may God bless him and grant him peace, said: there is nothing between the children of Adam and the devils except Bism Allah al-Rahman al-Rahim; so it is the concealed name
which يدلُّ upon that what after it is the Greatest Name, and it is Allah, because the Greatest Name is the Majesty, and it is the pole of the names, and to it
they return, and it is among the names like the proper name, because if you ask from al-Rahman, then you say Allah, and likewise the rest of the names are annexed to Him
and are known by the Majesty and the loftiness of His exaltedness. And there is for it an additional nobility over the names, and it is that if you remove from it the letter alif,
there remains for it “lillah,” and if you remove from it the first lam, there remains for it, and if you remove the second also, there remains “huwa”; so every letter
of it is قائم by itself, and it is not like that with other than it among the names, because if you remove a letter from them, its meaning is invalidated. And this name,
the Greatest, its letters have not been altered, for it is a nobility over the names and a proof that it is the honored essence, established in might and permanence. And for it
there is another nobility indicating the singular essence of lordship and indicating the unification of divinity, for its beginning is the alif, and it is the first of
the letters and the first of the numbers, and its end is the letter ha’, and it points to the unification of godhead, and it is not found in other than it among the
names, and it says by the basmalah of its state: I am the First and the Last and the Manifest and the Hidden. Then He followed it with the attribute of Rahmaniyyah. He تعالى said:
Say: call upon Allah or call upon al-Rahman; whichever you call upon, so He gave you the choice between saying O Allah or O Rahman, for He is the جامع of the two
Rahmaniyyah attributes and the noble speech. So if you wish the seeking of mercy, then say O Rahman, and it is the more specific, because God تعالى is the most specific
of the names and the greatest of them in agreement, so it is a رباني name. As for its explanation, it is that He brings things out from nonexistence into existence. And it has meanings
other than these, concerning which it is obligatory upon the one looking into them to withhold them from the fools, lest they reach to the doing of the reprehensible and the forbidden acts, and so it fall
with God like “he sold,” and when God تعالى intended by it disobedience— we seek refuge in God تعالى from His wrath—O God, do not make us among those who seek help
by Your names upon their disobedience. And this name has four letters: alif, lam, and ha’; for it are four natures, and the quarters
are four: east and west and north and south; and the angels of glorification are four: Jibril and Mikail and Israfil and Izra’il.
So Jibril is the possessor of the message to the sent ones, and the possessor of victory and subjugation, and by him God تعالى destroyed the disbelievers from the nations
. al-qidhah

**English:**
and the blowing. And Israfil is the possessor of the Trumpet and the blowing; for him are three blowings: the blowing of terror, as He تعالى said: “then those in
the heavens and those on the earth were terrified”; and the blowing of the swoon, as He تعالى said: “then those in the heavens and those on the earth swooned”; and the blowing
of resurrection, as He تعالى said: “then he will blow into it another time, and behold, they are standing, looking.” So for every blowing are affairs that are specific to it. And Izra’il,
peace be upon him, is entrusted with seizing the souls and their annihilation, and in it is the subduing of the tyrants and the cutting off of the remnant of the disbelievers and the arrogant, and in it is
the rest of the believer and his arrival to his Lord and his joy in what God has prepared for him of His generosity and His bounty and His pardon and His forgiveness. And Mikail,
peace be upon him, is entrusted with the provisions of the servants and their sustenance and the preservation of their existence, so there is not in the earth a sesame seed except that there is an assistant from
the angels entrusted with it for its owner. So for Jibril, peace be upon him, are countless assistants, and he has remembrances and works that differ from them. And these
four are kings of days, days being specific to them: for Jibril, peace be upon him, is Monday because it is cold, moist; and for Israfil, peace be upon him,
is Thursday, and it is hot, moist; and for Izra’il, peace be upon him, is Saturday because it is cold, moist, its place being earth
and death and annihilation. And for Mikail, peace be upon him, is Wednesday, and it is mixed from the four natures. And there are four awfaq
specific to them: the sevenfold for Jibril, peace be upon him, and the fourfold for Israfil, peace be upon him, and the threefold for Izra’il,
peace be upon him, and the eightfold for Mikail, peace be upon him. And these four awfaq, and this is their description as you see:
the form of the threefold for Izra’il, peace be upon him
the form of the fourfold for Israfil, peace be upon him

Know, may God grant me and you success in obedience to Him, that these awfaq have a tremendous influence in everything you want; so whoever reflects upon them finds them very correct,
by them he does whatever he wishes. And fear God, your Lord, in all your states. So if you desire a work from the works of the four awfaq, then write
its seal after counting it and its correctness, and add to it the name of the sought one; you will obtain what you want. As for the sevenfold, it is written on paper or pure white silver
on Monday at sunrise, the hour of the moon. If it is for a stone, then write it in the increase of the moon, and if

## Page 42

**Arabic (original):**
— ٣٦ —

كان في شرفه أو سعده سالماً من النحوس كان أبلغ، وإن أردت غير ذلك من الانتقام للأعداء أو ظلام
جبار فليكن القمر في المحاق والاحتراق متصلاً بزحل والمريخ، وإياك أن تفعله لغير مستحقه، والعفو
أولى، قال تعالى: {وأن تعفوا أقرب للتقوى}، وقال تعالى: {فمن عفا وأصلح فأجره على الله}، ودخن للخير
بالدختة الطيبة وللشر بضدها، فانظر فإن كان القمر في برج هوائي علق في الهواء، وإن كان في برج ناري
في النار، وإن كان في برج مائي ففي الماء أو ادفنه في قرب الماء، وإن أردت إرساله فيكون في قصبة
فارس مشمسة وأقرأ عليه ما يأتي، وإن كان في تراب ادفنه في التراب تحت عتبة بابه أو بابك إن أردت
جلبه إليك، وإن كان عظماً أجابك، وهذا ما تقرؤه عليه تقول: بسم الله الرحمن الرحيم، اللهم إني أسألك
بأسمائك الحسنى كلها الحمد لله التي إذا وضعت على شيء ذل وخضع، وإذا طلبت به الحسنة أدركت،
وإذا صرفت بهن السيئات صرفت، وبكلماتك التامة التي لو أن ما في الأرض من شجرة أقلام والبحر
يمده من بعده سبعة أبحر ما نفدت كلمات الله إن الله عزيز حكيم، يا كافي يا ولي يا رؤوف يا لطيف
يا رزاق يا ودود يا قيوم يا عليم يا واسع يا كريم يا وهاب يا باسط يا ذا الطول يا معطي يا مغني يا رحمن يا رحيم
يا غني يا مغيث يا حنان يا منان يا جواد يا حسن يا منتقم، اللهم أغنني بجلالك عن حرامك وبطاعتك عن
معصيتك وبفضلك عمن سواك يا أرحم الراحمين، وأسألك اللهم باسمك الذي لا إله إلا هو الجليل الرحمن
الرحيم اللطيف العظيم الرزاق الغفور المؤمن المهيمن المميت المجيب القريب السميع سريع
الكريم ذو الجلال والإكرام ذو الطول المنان، واعلم أن حامل هذه الأسماء وذاكرها تكرم أخلاقه
وتجود بالكرم والرحمة للناس به، ويشاهد من معاني اللطيف عجائب ويحصل له القبول ويحمد ظاهره
وباطنه لأن فيه اسم الله الأعظم الذي إذا دعي به أجاب وإذا سئل به أعطى، وهو من أعظم الأذكار
وأشرفها، ومن لازم على ذكرها كشف له عن أسرار عجيبة ويسر له المطلوب ورزق المرغوب في
الأمور عاجلة، ومن داوم على هذه الأسماء نصف الليل شاهد من العجائب على قدر همته ومداومتها
فتح الأسرار المكنونة، ولا يداوم على ذكرها أحد إلا ورأى من أمور العالم من الملكوت العلوي
وسخر له كل علم من الملائكة، وهي الكلمات التامات و [~] بدائع الأسرار، فمن ذكرها مع اسمه الكافي
وهو يتمنى شأناً له من حيث لا يدرى ولا يخطر بباله ولا يذكره أحد وهو في مرتبة واهية وهمته
تطلب أعلى من الأيسر الله تعالى الوصول إليها من غير تعب، ومن استدام ذكر الكافي والجامع على
شيء ضاع له وجده ورجع إليه ما فقد، واسمه التفويح لدفع المؤلم من الأمور العظام، والرؤوف
ذكر للحائفة من تتاه ملهوف إلا وجد الطمأنينة وسكن روعه، ومن داوم على ذكره إلى أن يغلب
عليه منه حال مع خلو المعدة من الطعام وأمسك النار بيده، قال لا ضره ولا تنفس على قدر يغلى يظل
غليانه بإذن الله تعالى، ويضيف إليه الحليم والرؤوف والمنان يا رب يا منان، وإذا كتب هذه الأسماء
في ساعة قمر وقابل من يخاف شره كفاه الله تعالى شره عند رؤيته، ولا يستديم هذه الأسماء الثلاثة
من غلبت عليه شهوته إلا أزال الله عنه ذلك، وللرجع ذلك كابصده من فوائد بسم الله الرحمن
الرحيم أنها لما نزلت أهز السموات بها من الملائكة وأهتز العرش لنزولها ونزل معها من الملائكة
ما لا يحصى عدده إلا الله وازدادت الملائكة إيماناً وتحركت الأفلاك وذلت لعظمتها الأملاك وكانت

**English:**
— 36 —

If it was in his honor or his fortune safe from the inauspicious, it was more eloquent, and if you desire other than that, from vengeance upon enemies or the darkness of a tyrant, then let the moon be in the waning and the combustion connected with Saturn and Mars, and beware that you do it for other than one deserving it, and pardon is more fitting. He تعالى said: {And that you pardon is nearer to piety}, and He تعالى said: {So whoever pardons and makes reconciliation, his reward is upon Allah}. And fumigate for the good with the good fumigation and for evil with its opposite. So look: if the moon is in an airy sign, suspend it in the air; and if it is in a fiery sign, then in the fire; and if it is in a watery sign, then in the water, or bury it near water; and if you desire sending it, then it will be in a sunlit reed of Faris, and recite upon it what follows; and if it is in earth, bury it in the earth beneath the threshold of his door or your door if you desire to bring him to you; and if it is bone, it will answer you. And this is what you recite upon it: In the name of Allah, the Merciful, the Compassionate. O Allah, I ask You by all Your most beautiful names. Praise be to Allah, which, if placed upon a thing, it becomes humbled and submissive, and if the good is sought by it, it is attained, and if the evil deeds are turned away by them, they are turned away, and by Your perfect words, that if what is on the earth of trees were pens and the sea were replenishing it after it with seven seas, the words of Allah would not be exhausted. Surely Allah is Mighty, Wise. O Sufficient, O Patron, O Compassionate, O Gentle, O Provider, O Loving, O Self-Subsisting, O Knowing, O Vast, O Generous, O Bestower, O Expander, O Possessor of bounty, O Giver, O Enricher, O Merciful, O Compassionate, O Rich, O Reliever, O Tender, O Bestower, O Bountiful, O Good, O Avenger. O Allah, enrich me by Your majesty from Your unlawful, and by Your obedience from Your disobedience, and by Your bounty from other than You, O Most Merciful of the merciful. And I ask You, O Allah, by Your name by which there is no god but He: the Majestic, the Merciful, the Compassionate, the Gentle, the عظیم, the Provider, the Forgiving, the Believer, the Guardian, the Slayer, the Responsive, the Near, the Hearing, the Swift, the Generous, Possessor of Majesty and Honor, Possessor of bounty, the Bestower. And know that the bearer of these names and the one who remembers them, his character is ennobled and he becomes generous with generosity and mercy toward people through them, and he witnesses from the meanings of the Gentle wonders, and acceptance is obtained for him, and his outward and inward are praised, because in it is the Greatest Name of Allah, by which if He is called upon He answers, and if He is asked by it He gives, and it is among the greatest remembrances and the most noble of them. And whoever persists in its remembrance, wondrous secrets are unveiled to him and the sought is made easy for him and the desired is provided in immediate matters. And whoever continues these names at midnight witnesses wonders according to the measure of his aspiration and his persistence, the opening of the hidden secrets, and no one persists in their remembrance except that he sees from the affairs of the world from the higher dominion and every knowledge from the angels is made subservient to him, and they are the perfect words and [~] marvels of secrets. So whoever remembers them with his sufficient name while he desires a matter for himself from where he does not know and it does not occur to his mind and no one mentions it to him, and he is in a weak rank and his aspiration seeks higher than the easier, Allah تعالى makes reaching it without toil. And whoever keeps up the remembrance of the Sufficient and the Gatherer upon something lost to him, he finds it and what he lost returns to him. And its name is التفويح for repelling the painful from the great matters, and the Compassionate is a remembrance for the one who is frightened from wandering; no distressed one but finds tranquility and his terror is calmed. And whoever persists in its remembrance until a state from it overcomes him, with the stomach empty of food, and holds the fire in his hand, he said: it does not harm him, and no breath upon a measure boils; its boiling remains by the permission of Allah تعالى. And he adds to it the Forbearing, the Compassionate, and the Bestower: O Lord, O Bestower. And if these names are written in an hour of the moon and one meets one whose evil he fears, Allah تعالى suffices him against his evil at the time of seeing him. And these three names are not kept up by one whom desire has overcome except that Allah removes that from him. And for the return, that is as I have intended. Among the benefits of Bismillah al-Rahman al-Rahim is that when it descended, the heavens were shaken by it from the angels, and the Throne trembled at its descent, and with it descended from the angels what none can count except Allah, and the angels increased in faith, and the spheres moved, and the kings were humbled to its greatness, and there were
